Resolve the remaining non-\'etale, non-nilpotent, and site-geometric open strata of Theorem~\ref{thm:postnikov-stepwise-proved} and Conjecture~\ref{conj:postnikov-stepwise} across arbitrary Grothendieck $(\infty, 1)$-topoi $\mathcal{X}$~\cite{Lurie09}: determine whether the representation $1$-gerbe $R(E_1) = 1_{\mathcal{X}/Y} \times_{\mathbf{Lien}^\kappa} \mathcal{M}_* \twoheadrightarrow 1_{\mathcal{X}/Y}$ splits beyond locally bounded-nilpotent bands and \'etale $\mathbf{Sh}_\infty(B)_{/W}$ topoi so that $0$-truncated effacement of $H^2_{\mathrm{gerbe}}(\mathcal{X}/Y, L(G))$~\cite{Giraud71} over global group objects $G \in \mathbf{Grp}(\tau_{\le 0}(\mathcal{X}/Y))$ suffices unconditionally for $(\mathrm{AC}_0 \Rightarrow \mathrm{AC}_1)$, characterize $0$-truncated effacement at all $n \ge 0$ via $(n+1)$-localic / hyperconnected site modalities~\cite[\S6.4.5]{Lurie09}, and classify non-hypercomplete $(\infty, 1)$-topoi testing the independence of Postnikov convergence from sequential $\mathrm{DC}$ in Theorem~\ref{thm:limit-residuum}.
\end{problem}

\begin{problem}[Cohesive Gauge Bridges and Differential Cohomological Descent]\label{prob:open-10}
Apply Schreiber's differential cohesive modality structure~\cite{Lawvere07,MoerdijkReyes91,Schreiber13} ($\CohesiveQuad$ together with $\mathrm{Im} \dashv \mathrm{Inf}$) inside an $(\infty, 1)$-topos $\mathcal{X}$ to extract exact CRM residuums bridging local Lie $\infty$-connections and flat curvature forms to global Chern--Weil classes, $B^n U(1)$ bundle gerbes, and the Simons--Sullivan differential cohomology fracture square~\cite{SimonsSullivan08,Schreiber13}.
\end{problem}

\subsection*{Horizon VI: Directed $(\infty, \infty)$-Localizations, Phantoms, and $\mathrm{CatSp}$}

\begin{problem}[Reflective Localization Boundaries Across the Four-Tier $(\infty, \infty)$-Ladder and \texttt{ICaTT} Adequacy]\label{prob:open-11}
Complete the residuum atlas across Ozornova--Rovelli--Walde and Gepner--Heine's strict four-tier chain inside $\mathrm{Cat}^R_{(\infty, \infty)} := \varprojlim_\kappa \mathrm{Cat}_{(\infty, n)}$~\cite{ORW26,GepnerHeine26},
\[
  \mathrm{Cat}^L_{(\infty, \infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^\omega_{(\infty, \infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^{\mathrm{coind}}_{(\infty, \infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^R_{(\infty, \infty)}
\]
(enforcing Simon Henry's erratum withdrawing Goldthorpe's earlier~\cite{Goldthorpe23} terminal-coalgebra claim on $\omega$-categories with weakly coinductive equivalences while retaining inductive initial algebras on $\mathrm{SymMon}^{\mathrm{Pres}}_\infty$, relative perfection $A_{\mathrm{perf}}$~\cite{Joyal85,BhattScholze22} without touching general prisms $(A, I)$, and $\mathbf{ICaTT}$ Grothendieck--Maltsiniotis clan localness at Tier~3 with $\mathrm{IDC}_\omega$ stream adequacy in Theorem~\ref{thm:coinductive-stream-choice}~\cite{BCM26,FinsterMimram17}). Following the negative resolution of Conjecture~C1 via the gaunt Noetherian rank-$\omega$ step-product $\mathcal{Q}$ (Theorems~\ref{thm:ordinal-rank-completeness} and~\ref{thm:classifier-scc-trapping}--\ref{thm:step-product-counterexample}, \texttt{OrdinalRankCompleteness.lean}, \texttt{StepProductPhantom.lean}, and Proposition~\ref{prop:lali-ceiling}), identify natural non-abelian internal conditions weakening $1$-acyclicity that force $\mathcal{C} \in \mathrm{Cat}^L_{(\infty, \infty)}$ inside $\mathrm{Cat}_{\le \omega}$.
\end{problem}

\begin{problem}[Directed Stabilization in $\mathrm{CatSp}$ Without Group-Completion Collapse]\label{prob:open-12}
Classify all $\pi_{\le 0}$-induced group-completion and $\Sigma_{\mathrm{red}} \dashv \Omega_{\mathrm{dir}}$ stabilization residuums in $\mathrm{CatSp} := \mathrm{Sp}(\mathrm{Cat}^R_*)$ across $\mathrm{CatSp}^L \subsetneq \mathrm{CatSp}^\omega \subsetneq \mathrm{CatSp}^{\mathrm{coind}} \subsetneq \mathrm{CatSp}^R$ (Sections~\ref{subsec:crm-discovery-engine-inf-inf} and~\ref{subsec:excision-catsp};~\cite{Masuda26,Heine26,Geiss26}), extending the non-collapsing deloopings $\mathbf{B}^\infty(\mathbf{FinSet}^{\simeq})$ and $\mathbf{B}^\infty(M_4^\#)$ to $4$-manifold cobordism categorical spectra without Wall stable diffeomorphism collapse or Giraud strict-initiality collapse.
\end{problem}

\subsection*{Horizon VII: Borel Complexity, Smooth $4$-Manifolds, and Condensed Tail Residua}

\begin{problem}[Light Condensed $\mathrm{Ext}^1_{\mathrm{CondAb}}$ vs.\ Definable $\varprojlim^1_{\mathrm{def}}$ on Exotic $\mathbb{R}^4$'s]\label{prob:open-13}
Compare the non-splitting light condensed torsor class $[q] \neq 0 \in \mathrm{Ext}^1_{\mathrm{CondAb}}(\mathcal{Q}_{E_0}, \bigoplus_{\mathbb{N}} \mathbb{F}_2)$ in $\mathbf{Sh}(\mathrm{Prof}_{\aleph_0})$ from the Double-Solvent Theorem (Theorem~\ref{thm:double-solvent-r4}, Chapter~\ref{ch:base-fiber}, Section~\ref{sec:exotic-r4-e0}) with Gompf--Panagiotopoulos's definable derived limit $\varprojlim^1_{\mathrm{def}}$ along compact exhaustions $K_0 \Subset K_1 \Subset \cdots \subset \mathbb{R}^4$~\cite{GompfPanagiotopoulos26,BergfalkSmythe25}, testing whether condensed $\mathrm{Ext}^1_{\mathrm{CondAb}}$ yields a complete invariant distinguishing radial from non-radial exotic $\mathbb{R}^4$'s between Freedman's topological collapse and $\mathrm{ZFC}$ Vitali splitting---and further formalizing the parallel with the $(\infty,\infty)$-step-product phantom $X_\bullet \in RL(\mathcal{Q}) \setminus \mathcal{Q}$ (Remark~\ref{rem:step-product-e0-twin}).
\end{problem}

\begin{problem}[Pro-Oligomorphic Blindness and Turbulent Classifiers Beyond $E_{S_\infty}$]\label{prob:open-14}
Calibrate the Borel complexity transition separating Rung~4 countably classifiable moduli ($E_\infty \le_B E \le_B E_{S_\infty}$, reduced via $S_\infty$ logic actions~\cite{BeckerKechris96,BergfalkSmythe25} to nominal / Fra\"iss\'e quotient stacks over $\mathbf{Nom} \simeq \mathbf{Cont}(\mathrm{Sym}(\mathbb{N}))$) from Rung~5 turbulent moduli ($\not\le_B E_{S_\infty}$, such as complex $n$-manifolds for $n \ge 2$ up to biholomorphism, Table~\ref{tab:crm-borel-dictionary};~\cite{HjorthKechris00,Hjorth00,GompfPanagiotopoulos26,Kihara26}): determine what condensed, cohesive, or $(\infty, 1)$-topos invariants replace continuous $\mathbf{Cont}(\mathrm{Aut}(\mathcal{M}))$ classifying functors once pro-oligomorphic blindness collapses every definable nominal invariant.
\end{problem}

\subsection*{Horizon VIII: Algebraic Set Theory, Proper-Class Unraveling, and Universe Fixed Points}

\begin{problem}[Explicit Class-Coalgebra Residuum of Borel Determinacy]\label{prob:open-15}
Extract the explicit class-level obstruction invariant $\mathrm{Res}(\kappa_{\mathrm{Det}})$ blocking small strategy descent on Tony Martin's~\cite{Martin75} proper-class inductive unraveling coalgebras inside Joyal--Moerdijk~\cite{JoyalMoerdijk95} $(\mathcal{C}, \mathcal{S})$ (Chapter~\ref{ch:sets-residuum}, Remark~\ref{rem:martin-borel-coalgebra}), calibrating the sharp pincer between Power-Set-free $\mathsf{ZFC}^-$ (whose provability of Borel Determinacy breaks at $\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^0_4$ in minimal or non-standard $\beta$-models lacking $\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{N})$, in contrast to full ambient $H(\aleph_1) \subset V \models \mathsf{ZFC}$~\cite{Friedman71,MontalbanShore12,GitmanHamkinsJohnstone16}, Proposition~\ref{prop:pointillist-continuum}) and Replacement-free $\mathsf{ZC}$ / $\mathsf{ETCS} \simeq \mathsf{Mac}$ (whose provability of Borel Determinacy breaks at $\boldsymbol{\Sigma}^0_{\omega+1}$ in non-standard or minimal models of $\mathsf{ZC}$~\cite{Friedman71}, while true ambient $V_{\omega+\omega} \subset V \models \mathsf{ZFC}$ inherits full Borel Determinacy even as $\{\mathcal{P}^n(\mathbb{N})\}_{n \in \mathbb{N}} \notin \mathcal{S}$ blocks Martin's inductive unraveling inside $(V_{\omega+\omega}, \mathrm{Def}(V_{\omega+\omega}))$, Proposition~\ref{prop:etcs-colimit-escape}).
\end{problem}

\begin{problem}[Class-Theoretic Calibration of the Four-Tier $(\infty, \infty)$- Localization Ladder]\label{prob:open-16}
Calibrate Ozornova--Rovelli--Walde and Gepner--Heine's four-tier reflective localization chain $\mathrm{Cat}^L_{(\infty,\infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^\omega_{(\infty,\infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^{\mathrm{coind}}_{(\infty,\infty)} \subsetneq \mathrm{Cat}^R_{(\infty,\infty)}$ \cite{ORW26,GepnerHeine26} and Goldthorpe's~\cite{Goldthorpe23} inductive $\Lambda$-iterated enrichment initial algebras along $\mathsf{GBC} \subsetneq \mathsf{NBG} \subsetneq \mathsf{NBG}+\mathsf{ETR}_{\mathrm{Ord}} \subsetneq \mathsf{KM}$ (and $\mathsf{GBC}+\mathsf{ETR}_{\mathrm{Ord}}$)~\cite{Gitman20,Williams18} without Grothendieck universes, testing whether $\alpha = 0$ stabilization on locally finite-dimensional $\mathrm{Cat}_{\le \omega}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:ordinal-rank-completeness}) drops the metatheoretic cost from $\mathsf{ETR}_{\mathrm{Ord}}$ down to $\mathsf{GBC}$ below the $\mathrm{Con}(\mathsf{ZFC})$ reflection threshold already crossed at $\mathsf{ETR}_\omega$ (distinct from impredicative $\mathrm{Con}(\mathsf{GBC}+\mathsf{ETR}_{\mathrm{Ord}})$ at $\mathsf{KM}$).
\end{problem}

\begin{problem}[Leray Transgression for $\pi_{\mathrm{Set}}$ and Univalent Coalgebras $\nu\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{Anima}}$]\label{prob:open-17}
Construct a quantitative transgression differential on $\pi_{\mathrm{Set}}$ (Definition~\ref{def:set-projection-fibration}) governing when vertical class torsors ($\mathbf{Card} \hookrightarrow \mathbf{Set}$ splitting, Proposition~\ref{prop:large-equiv-splitting}) remain $\mathcal{L}_\in$-conservative~\cite{Felgner71} versus leaking small-set theorems over $\mathsf{ZC}$~\cite{Glazer23} (Theorem~\ref{thm:glazer-nonconservativity}) and $\mathsf{ZFC}_{\neg\mathrm{Reg}}$~\cite{Felgner76}, and construct the d\'ecalage functor from the terminal coalgebra $\nu\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{Anima}}$ of non-well-founded univalent multisets ($\Omega(\nu\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{Anima}}, [q,q]) \cong \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$, Remark~\ref{rem:nu-anima-fossil}) to Rieger--Bernays permutation topoi $\mathbf{Cont}(\mathrm{Sym}(Q_0))$~\cite{Bernays54,Rieger57,Boffa69} (Proposition~\ref{prop:boffa-torsors}). Because naive model-space pullbacks fail to preserve subclass separation (\texttt{naive\_ pullback\_ breaks\_ subclass\_ separation}), lift the transgression onto a coupled two-level architecture---pairing Macro $\Sigma^1_0$-Morleyized classifying topoi $\gamma_I \colon \mathcal{E}[\mathbb{T}_{\mathrm{Class}}] \to \mathcal{E}[\mathbb{T}_{\mathrm{Set}}]$ whose compactly supported cohomology $H^0_c(U_{\mathrm{leak}}; \mathbb{Z})$ records syntactic reflection leaks ($\mathsf{ETR}_{\mathrm{Ord}}$, $\mathsf{KM}$) with Micro internal $\mathsf{AST}$ class-forcing sheaves $\gamma_\Sigma$ isolating predicative $\mathcal{S}$-distributivity leaks (Glazer over $\mathsf{ZC}$, Felgner over $\mathsf{ZFC}_{\neg\mathrm{Reg}}$).
\end{problem}

\section{Machine-Checked Lean~4 Formalization Ledger (\texttt{HomotopyCRM})}\label{subsec:lean4-verification-ledger}

To anchor these categorical, algebraic, and combinatorial separations against vacuous definitions or hidden metamathematical assumptions, core components of Chapters~\ref{ch:foundations}, \ref{ch:delta-rings}, \ref{ch:base-fiber}, \ref{ch:higher-crm}, \ref{ch:realizability-sites}, and~\ref{ch:sets-residuum} are formalized in Lean~4 over \texttt{mathlib4} inside the \texttt{HomotopyCRM} library under a strict four-layer kernel verification gate (concrete \texttt{Nontrivial} carrier inhabitation, bidirectional positive/negative predicate separation, stock \texttt{mathlib4} typeclass projection with derived boundary lemmas, and \texttt{\#guard\_msgs in \#print axioms} permitting only \texttt{propext}, \texttt{Classical.choice}, and \texttt{Quot.sound} with zero \texttt{sorry}, custom axioms, or \texttt{opaque} terms; 21,166 verified lines across eleven production modules, audited via 225 \texttt{\#guard\_msgs in \#print axioms} checks):
\begin{itemize}
  \item \texttt{HomotopyCRM/ InfinityResiduum.lean} (949 lines, 20 checks): Formalizes Chapter~1 (\S\ref{sec:finset-infinity}) finite dynamical lassos \texttt{LassoCarrier p q} / \texttt{lassoDynSys}, non-initiality in $\mathbf{FinSet}$ (\texttt{isEmpty\_ hom\_ of\_ fintype\_ to\_ succ\_ lasso}), coherent transition maps \texttt{lassoTransition}, profinite surjection \texttt{profiniteLassoToZHat\_ surjective} ($\widehat{\mathbb{N}}_{\mathrm{lasso}} \twoheadrightarrow \widehat{\mathbb{Z}}$) with non-natural $-1$ separation (\texttt{zHatToProfiniteLasso\_ neg\_ one\_ not\_ mem\_ range\_ nat}), Pigeonhole triviality vs.\ nominal atom crossing (\texttt{dedekindResiduum\_ eq\_ empty\_ of\_ fintype}, \texttt{nominalDedekindResiduum\_ eq\_ empty}, \texttt{not\_ hasFiniteSupportMap\_ nat\_ succ}), and biproduct-tail cokernels (\texttt{TailCokernel}, \texttt{subsingleton\_ tailCokernel\_ of\_ fintype}, \texttt{nontrivial\_ tailCokernel\_ nat}).
  \item \texttt{HomotopyCRM/ DeltaRingPerfection.lean} (2,141 lines, 28 checks): Formalizes Goldthorpe's slice endofunctor \texttt{sliceEndofunctor F c}, Lambek's isomorphism \texttt{lambek\_ isIso}, fixed-point coalgebra equivalence (\texttt{FixedPoint CoalgebraUnder}, \texttt{theoremA\_ homEquiv}), sequential colimit relative perfection $A_{\mathrm{perf}} = \varinjlim_\phi A$ with shift chirality $\phi_{A_{\mathrm{perf}}} = s^{-1}$ (\texttt{theoremB\_ natEndo\_ app\_ eq\_ shift\_ inv}), \texttt{DeltaRing2Cat} and \texttt{DeltaStructure} with Joyal's integral additive cofactor \texttt{delta\_add} (\texttt{DeltaRing2.toDeltaStructure}, $C_2(x, y) = -xy$), concrete $2$-torsion witness $R_2 = \mathbb{Z}[\eta]/(2\eta, \eta^2)$ with rigid automorphism group (\texttt{r2\_ aut\_ unique}), $\mathrm{Der}_\phi$-pseudo-torsor (\texttt{deltaRing2\_ sub\_ frobDerivation2}), non-isomorphic lifts (\texttt{r2Delta0\_ not\_ iso\_ r2Delta1}), and stage-$i=2$ Nygaard filtration separation on \texttt{DualInt} ($\mathbb{Z}[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^2)$).

  \item \texttt{HomotopyCRM/ OrdinalRankCompleteness.lean} (1,810 lines, 19 checks): Formalizes universe-safe globular state coalgebras \texttt{GlobularInfSystem S}, transfinite ordinal invertibility (\texttt{IsEquiv}, \texttt{IsCoindEquiv}, \texttt{IsOrdinalInv}, \texttt{IsOmegaInv}), Theorem~\ref{thm:ordinal-rank-completeness} (\texttt{theorem9A\_ ordinal\_ rank\_ completeness}, \texttt{theorem9A\_ omega\_ completeness}, \texttt{orw\_ question558\_ stabilizes\_ at\_ zero}), unified rank separation $0 < 1 < 2 < \omega < \omega + 1$ in \texttt{OmegaTowerState} alongside \texttt{CoindWalkState} (\texttt{Checks. strict\_ four\_ tier\_ equivalence\_ separation}), and cell-height stabilization (\texttt{globularSim\_ stabilization\_ above\_ height}, specialized in \texttt{prop11A\_ lali\_ stabilization\_ above\_ ceiling}) with sharp $+2$ join-cone boundary \texttt{JoinSuspState.joinCone K} (\texttt{prop11A\_ ceiling\_ sharp\_ at\_ max\_ add\_ two}).

three-mode homotopy-fiber defect witnesses separating $(-1)$-connected inhabitation from $(-2)$-contractibility on $\mathbb{Z} \twoheadrightarrow \mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ and pro-tower phantoms (\texttt{intToZMod2TypeMode\_ sectionSpace\_ nontrivial}, \texttt{not\_ intToZMod2TypeMode\_ contractible}, \texttt{isEmpty\_ intToZMod2AbMode\_ sectionSpace}, \texttt{profiniteLassoTowerMode\_ hasPhantom}, \texttt{stepProductTowerMode\_ hasPhantom}); Doctrinal Philosophies, Inquisitor's Impossibility, the Choice-free finite presheaf separations on \texttt{M1Monoid}, the $3$-element V-poset, $D_4$, and the $2$-object Freyd cover $(\mathbf{B}(\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}))_\bot$, plus the classical-\texttt{Type} $4$-topos Pareto Schism $\{\mathrm{Proj}_{\mathrm{sub}}, \mathrm{Localic}\}$ and $3^k 2^m$ slice Lawvere--Tierney enumeration (\texttt{DoctrinalPhilosophy}, \texttt{inquisitor\_ impossibility\_ casuistry}, \texttt{monoid\_ proj1\_ iff\_ rightZero}, \texttt{m1\_ projSub\_ and\_ not\_ localic}, \texttt{vPoset\_ localic\_ not\_ proj1}, \texttt{diamond\_ proj1\_ not\_ projSub}, \texttt{not\_ catPresheafIAC\_ m1Cat}, \texttt{not\_ catPresheafIAC\_ vPosetOpCat}, \texttt{twoGroupCat\_ iac\_ and\_ not\_ ac}, \texttt{freydCover\_ dogma\_ banishment}, \texttt{choiceConcl\_ ne\_ extProj}, \texttt{pareto\_ incomparable\_ projSub\_ localic}, \texttt{M1SliceLTT. m1\_ slice\_ lt\_ count}); Chapter~\ref{ch:delta-rings}'s two-stage Mode~(iii) $\delta$-ring lift tower over \texttt{FrobLiftRing2} culminating in $16$ rigidified lifts and $6$ $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{F}_2)$-orbits on $R_{2,2}$ (\texttt{empty\_ deltaLift\_ zmod2}, \texttt{empty\_ deltaLift\_ zmod4}, \texttt{isEmpty\_ deltaRing2\_ zmod4}, \texttt{empty\_ deltaLift\_ R2Idemp}, \texttt{r2\_ lift\_ card\_ eq\_ two}, \texttt{r2\_ lifts\_ non\_ iso}, \texttt{r22\_ lift\_ card\_ eq\_ sixteen}, \texttt{r22\_ iso\_ orbits\_ eq\_ six}); and Chapter~\ref{ch:base-fiber}'s $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_5) \twoheadrightarrow A_5$ Schur transgression injection (\texttt{commutator\_ sl2\_ f5\_ eq\_ top}, \texttt{sl2\_ f5\_ leray\_ transgression\_ injective}).
\end{itemize}

\section{Master Reference Atlas of Categorical Reverse Mathematics}

\begin{center}\emph{[Summary ledger table omitted in blueprint edition; see Royal Octavo monograph PDF.]}\end{center}

\section{Epilogue: Mathematical Truth as Obstruction Invariant}\label{sec:epilogue-truth}

To read mathematics through the lens of Categorical Reverse Mathematics is to abandon a one-dimensional picture of axiomatic truth in favor of structural covariance. What separates intuitionistic arithmetic from classical analysis, constructive locales from Stone spatiality, finite-rank $(\infty,\infty)$-categories from Postnikov completeness, or compact smooth $n$-manifolds from radial exotic $\mathbb{R}^4$'s is never a single undifferentiated ``extra axiom.'' Rather, every theorem $B$ relative to a weaker core $A$ over a doctrinal comparison triple $\mathfrak{T} = (\mathcal{D}_0 \supseteq \mathcal{D}_1, \kappa) \in \mathrm{Dom}(\mathrm{Res})$ projects a sharp geometric, cohomological, or modal shadow---the residuum $\mathrm{Res}(\kappa)$---measuring precisely where ambient symmetry or missing completeness blocks global gluing.

Across nine chapters, five canonical anchor witnesses have materialized this obstruction calculus on the page and in the kernel of Lean~4: the odd-finset parity torsor $[\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{odd}}] = \delta(1) \neq 0 \in H^1(\mathbf{Nom}; \mathrm{EvenFinset})$ blocking external projectivity of $1_{\mathbf{Nom}}$ even in a Boolean atomic topos; the $2$-torsion $\delta$-ring $R_2 = \mathbb{Z}[\eta]/(2\eta, \eta^2)$ ($\mathbb{Z}\oplus\mathbb{F}_2$ additively) and torsion-free \texttt{DualInt} pinning relative slice perfection $A_{\mathrm{perf}}$ precisely against the Nygaard filtration; the gaunt Noetherian rank-$\omega$ step-product $\mathcal{Q} \subset \prod_{k \ge 1}\mathcal{A}_k$ whose absorbing off-diagonals birth the pure Postnikov phantom $X_\bullet \in RL(\mathcal{Q}) \setminus \mathcal{Q}$ refuting Conjecture~C1; the directed facet cycles $F_n$ erecting an uncrossable $n \ge 3$ cubical amalgamation wall past binary two-clause flow-convexity; and the light condensed smooth-$4$-manifold modulus $\mathcal{Q}_{E_0} = 2^{\mathbb{N}} \sslash E_0$ with CAR convolution algebra $\mathrm{UHF}(2^\infty)$ ($K_0 \cong \mathbb{Z}[\tfrac{1}{2}]$), whose $\mathrm{Ext}^1_{\mathrm{CondAb}}$ tail class mirrors $X_\bullet$ between Freedman topological collapse and $\mathrm{ZFC}$ Vitali splitting.

Backing these theorems stands the 21,166-line, eleven-module \texttt{HomotopyCRM} library in Lean~4 (\texttt{leanprover/lean4:v4.28.0}) over \texttt{mathlib4}, audited under a strict four-layer kernel verification gate with zero \texttt{sorry}, zero custom axioms, and zero \texttt{opaque} declarations across 225 \texttt{\#guard\_msgs in \#print axioms} checks. Whether the seventeen open problems across Horizons~I--VIII yield new Pareto frontiers, Weihrauch--Lawvere--Tierney dualities, higher cubical invariants, or condensed exotic-$\mathbb{R}^4$ obstructions, the guiding principle remains unchanged: mathematical necessity is never an accident of syntax, but the geometry of the obstructions that vanish when universes align. Dissecting ``$\mathbf{Sets}$'' itself as a double-clamped locus inside $(\mathcal{C}, \mathcal{S})$---bridging to univalent universes $(\mathcal{X}, \mathcal{U})$ and a Volume~II elementary class-anima horizon $(\mathcal{C}_\infty, \mathcal{S}_\infty)$---thus completes the foundational arc of Volume~I and launches Volume~II (\emph{Universes, Classes, and Higher Fixed Points}).
